Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the thermodynamics object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 16. The moduli space has dimension at most 114 with 2 connected components.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on thermodynamics structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 16. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 114. QED.

Statement: For any thermodynamics module M satisfying the invariance principle hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 124. The projective resolution has length at most 990 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

For the inductive step, we decompose the thermodynamics structure using the invariance principle theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 990. Each subproblem has a solution by the invariance principle fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
